- Day: 56 (session 2) — Big Phase 3 multiplayer expansion, built in 5 sequential pieces (Model: Opus, per the Model Selection Rule — new real-time architecture and matchmaking logic). **(1) Tournaments restructured** into 3 clean parts on the entry screen: 🌙 Daily Tournament (existing 8PM Knockout/10PM Round Robin scheduled signup), ⚡ Auto-Match (new), 👥 Invite Friends (existing create-and-share-code flow). "League + Playoffs" renamed to "Round Robin Tournament" everywhere user-facing (lobby badge, group-stage title, champion screen, push notifications) — internal identifiers (`type:'league'`, `createLeagueTournament()`) untouched. **(2) Tournament auto-matchmaking**: `createTournament()`/`createLeagueTournament()` gained an `isPublic` param; new `findPublicTournament(type, bracketSize)` mirrors `findPublicMatch()`'s proven pattern — finds an open public tournament of the same type/size and joins it, or creates one and waits. **(3) Multiplayer Wordle** built from scratch: new `wordleMatches/{roomCode}` collection, own page/engine (`wordle-multiplayer.html`/`.js`) rather than retrofitting the quiz engine, since "one shared mystery player, up to 6 private guesses each over a single 120s clock" doesn't fit the quiz engine's fixed-10-question phase model. Reuses `comparePlayer()`/`fillRow()`/`buildGuessRows()`/`displayValue()` from `wordle.js` unchanged; guesses live in a per-player subcollection readable only by the guesser and the host. Scoring: guess 1 = 100pts down to guess 6 = 20pts, 0 if unsolved. **Caught and fixed a real bug during build**: `wordle.js`'s `window.onload` would have silently consumed an entry from the single-player shuffle bag every time the multiplayer page loaded — neutralized by overwriting `window.onload` before it could fire. **(4) Multiplayer Ranking Challenge** built the same way: new `rankingMatches/{roomCode}` collection, own page/engine. Same 4 challenges for the whole room (generated once by the host from the real `ranking-challenges.js` pool), 10s per round, `answering → standings → next` mirroring the quiz battle phase pattern. Deliberately does NOT load `ranking.js` directly — it registers a `DOMContentLoaded` handler that would silently start a real single-player round (and a real countdown that could self-write XP) against DOM elements the new page doesn't have; the small set of pure functions actually needed (challenge generation, scoring, shuffling) are duplicated instead, clearly marked as such. The host recomputes each player's score itself from their submitted order rather than trusting a client-reported value. **Caught a real bug during build**: `ranking.html` has no separate `ranking.css` file — its styling is entirely inline in its own `<head>` — the first draft linked a file that doesn't exist, which would have shipped a totally unstyled page; caught before commit, the needed rules copied into the new page directly. **(5) Battle Friends game-type picker**: clicking Battle Friends now first shows a picker (IPL/Test/ODI/T20/Mixed quiz, Wordle Battle, Ranking Battle, Speed Mode, Last One Standing) before the existing Create Room/Join/Duel/Find Match screen. Wordle/Ranking route straight to their new dedicated pages. The quiz options set `chosenCategory`/`chosenMode`, read by `createRoom()` (now filters `questionIds` to that category, falling back to the full mixed bank if a category doesn't have enough questions) and `findPublicMatch()` (now only matches players wanting the same category+mode, via a client-side filter so the query still needs no composite index). Tournament's own `?matchId=` deep-link flow is untouched. All 5 pieces verified live via mocked Firestore calls (same no-real-account-needed pattern as every other Phase 3 feature before its first live playtest): tournament section rendering + auto-match candidate filtering (own-room/wrong-size/full-room all correctly excluded, earliest valid candidate joined, correct fallback-to-create when none exist); Wordle's full guess flow, host-side scoring aggregation, leaderboard XSS-safety, and host-migration clock math (including the overdue-clamps-to-zero case); Ranking's challenge rendering, submit flow, host-side score recomputation (5/5, 3/5, 0/5 across 3 mock players, correctly sorted), round-advance logic, and drag-reorder shift math; and the picker's category-filtered question selection (`pickQuestionIndexes('ipl')` returns exactly 10 IPL-tagged questions) plus category+mode-aware public matchmaking. Zero console errors across every check. **`firestore.rules` needs to be republished in the Firebase console before any of Tournaments' auto-match, Wordle Battle, or Ranking Battle will actually work for a real user** — same standing lesson as every previous rules change in this project; committing the file changes nothing on its own. **Not yet tested: a real multi-account playthrough of any of the 3 new pieces** — same category of gap as every other Phase 3 feature before its first live test.

Mohammad Rizwan's bowling style needed a direct check of ESPNcricinfo's own dedicated bowling-stats page (via the browser, since a broad search wrongly suggested "right-arm medium") — the page showed no bowling section at all for him, confirming a genuine 0-ball wicketkeeper-batter record like Rishabh Pant/Sanju Samson, so NA. Kamran Akmal and Sohail Tanvir both confirmed with real 2008 Rajasthan Royals IPL history (Akmal: 5 matches including the final; Tanvir: Purple Cap, 22 wickets, including a then-record 6/14 vs Chennai Super Kings) — both from the brief pre-ban window, consistent with the standing precedent for that era. All other 16 players in this batch debuted 2009 or later and get empty `iplTeams` arrays per the well-established informal exclusion of Pakistani players from the IPL, spot-checked directly for a few (Mohammad Amir, Shadab Khan, Ahmed Shehzad, Iftikhar Ahmed, Saim Ayub) rather than assumed for all. Verified live: structural check passed on all 457 players (zero missing fields, zero duplicates, all bowlingStyle/format/role values valid against their respective allowed lists, all 20 new names found), `comparePlayer()` tested on Mohammad Amir vs Ahmed Shehzad confirming correct green/grey logic (green on country/debutYear/format/iplTeams/iccTrophies since both debuted 2009, All-format, no IPL, and 2 ICC trophies each; grey on role/battingStyle/bowlingStyle), and a live guess submitted for Khawaja Nafay in the actual game UI via real click/type events — the rendered row (PAK/WK/R/R-OB/2026/T20/—/0) matched the verified data exactly, attempts counter decremented 6→5 correctly, zero console errors. **Pakistan T20I is now fully complete under the two-round rule (20/20: 18 Round 1 + 2 Round 2)** — per the FIXED TRAVERSAL ORDER, the next Wordle cell is **South Africa Test**.

## HOW TO START EACH DAY

Start a new chat each day rather than continuing an old one. CLAUDE.md already carries everything that matters (build status, rules, feature list), so a fresh chat loses nothing — and it keeps daily usage limits from being eaten up by reprocessing many days of old conversation. Only continue the same chat if doing a second session on the same day, shortly after the first.

Begin every chat session inside this project with:
"Starting Day [number] of CricTakkar. Yesterday we [what you did]. Today let's [what's next]."

---

## WHEN TO UPDATE THESE INSTRUCTIONS

Only update these instructions when:
- A full Phase is completed (update Current Build Status section only)
- A major feature is added or removed from the plan
- A tool in the tech stack changes
- A permanent rule is added (like the Question Quality Rule above)

Do NOT update for daily progress — that goes in your personal Google Doc diary.
